# V1 to V2 Operational Parity: October 1, 2026

## Remaining L0 Campaign: October 3, 2026

The campaign starts from delivered DEV
`42b05955c68dd8ae168e67c97a713cfc4d792086`. The four committed parity artifacts
remain the frozen starting evidence; no full audit, new row, L1 implementation,
Storefront or V1 modification is authorized.

After read-only fetch, **CURRENT_V1_MAIN** is
`fc46a73f6af7bb485e6dbc309175c7c80dec8771`. The old audited V1 remains
`d4f4c6b8706f0f7a2768d77054efb9b2dc28633e`. Both detached reference worktrees
are read-only and pristine. A narrow Git delta, not a 277-intent re-audit,
identified a material FUL-11 change in `shippingContext.ts`: an Order can override
the Customer blind-shipping default; explicit Customer or Custom sender source
is distinct from Ship To, incomplete Custom never falls back, and combined Orders
with incompatible sender details/sources fail. Historical unspecified sender
source preserves the Customer fallback. The inspected Quote, Portal, Production,
Billing and accounting owner paths have no delta. Presentation/note changes are
not new L0 requirements.

The user has approved three former decision gates:

- BILL-16: issued Invoice PDFs represent historical issued evidence; subsequent
  payments/refunds/credits/balance never rewrite the document. Current financial
  UI/account views remain separate. No Statement L1 work is included.
- ACCESS-05: ordinary mutation cannot remove/demote the last active structural
  Owner/Admin recovery authority. Capability roles cannot substitute. Preserve
  Owner and Platform Developer recovery paths without a custom-role backdoor.
- ACCESS-12: Sales owns publication; Portal shows the last explicitly
  sent/published immutable Quote revision. Staff edits to a newer internal
  revision do not leak; explicit resend advances visibility and retains history.

Six isolated Sol High lanes are assigned in the reconciliation ledger. Astra
High nonauthor reviewers must attempt to disprove every substantive package.
Shared API/composition, operation-request contracts, migrations and all audit
accounting remain coordinator-owned. No xhigh override is applied globally.

Source closures require focused negative, authorization, tenant, replay and
rollback evidence, independent review and full canonical/build gates. New Run
creation requires a genuine two-independent-connection native PostgreSQL proof;
PGlite alone is insufficient. Lane F is checking safely available disposable
infrastructure and starts with read-only deployed version/health/readiness.
Targeted writes require an approved disposable-QA manifest and deployed
REQUIRED_COMMIT (or verified descendant). QuickBooks stays disconnected; no
carrier purchase, real admin damage or valuable prior fixture mutation.

Polling after reviewed DEV milestones is no faster than once per minute; other
source work continues while deployment is pending. Until the relevant exact
workflow is checked, each source milestone is **Deployable but not yet
live-validated**. Stop once these ten rows are closed or all safe source is
complete with only genuinely unavailable external validation remaining. Do not
continue into L1.

Independent scope review `ses_efde9f04dffexplG4F2jy62IY1` confirmed the approved
policies and constrained their interpretation in the ledger. Receipt keys remain
tenant/operation/request, not actor-scoped; each owner reauthorizes disclosure
and replay. The structural floor is tenant-specific, not satisfied by a global
developer or cloned capability role. Publication never broadens Portal identity
and uncertain send cannot advance customer visibility. Historical Invoice identity,
addresses and branding are frozen along with money, with no fabricated legacy
evidence. Native proof and Sales conversion acceptance are workflow-specific and
do not disable unrelated accepted fulfillment or existing Run output. Disabled or
incomplete source is not an external-only closure. These are restrictive scope
clarifications, not implementation acceptance or additional feature authority.

### Recovery Verification

At source `be11ae6b`, all eleven `npm run v2:validate` gates passed: 99/99 Jest
suites, 1,121/1,121 tests, zero pending/skipped in those executed suites, 288 core
entries and 98 UI entries. These counters are not additive assertion totals.
Foundation/server builds and production UI build passed with a sanitized
environment; the build command explicitly sets `NODE_ENV=production`.

All 59 unique implementation/test/metadata blobs match the accepted source
packages, except the intentional union of two reviewed classifier registrations.
Import debt remains 81 instances/81 fingerprints. SQL debt decreases from 193/173
to 191/171 by retiring two actual Stripe-adapter writes into Billing-owned
operations. No scanner, guard, assertion budget or ownership rule was relaxed.

Migration `0299_v2_sales_quote_delivery_prepared_evidence` is the only new
migration, idx 300 and timestamp 1790827200003. It adds nullable historical
prepared evidence, requires versioned evidence for new attempts, checks identity
correspondence and prevents rewrite/backfill. All 294 prior SQL/journal entries
and their protected digest are unchanged; the new integrity frontier is 295.
Actual 0229/0299 SQL and catalog readiness executed in PGlite with 29 assertions;
an independent 60-check probe also exercised the real persistence helper.
Production and Payments actual-adapter SQL fixtures also passed.

Five DB-guarded suites, four QA rehearsals and one manual category were not run.
The guarded deployment HTTP test attempt stopped at the intentional dotenv
guard before tests; it was not bypassed or recategorized. Native two-client
PostgreSQL, provider, physical-printer and new DEV-live acceptance remain unrun.
The initial stale fixture/source assertions and the real frozen-Job-Label and
Refund-binding regressions were corrected and independently re-reviewed before
integration. The final canonical rerun passed without excluded failing tests.

Parity verdict: **NO**. Deployment label for every delivered milestone:
**Deployable but not yet live-validated**. Final delivery reruns gates/builds on
the final documentation commit, verifies fresh DEV ancestry, pushes only that
exact SHA to `refs/heads/dev`, and checks the remote ref. The completion report
records the final receipt. No deployment polling, MAIN/PROD promotion, new live
fixture mutation or Storefront work is part of this campaign.

The audited V1 reference remains `d4f4c6b8`. Recovery fetch observed an external
MAIN advancement to `fc46a73f`; this campaign neither edited nor pushed MAIN and
did not substitute that ref for the pinned audit. The unrelated caller checkout
and other worktrees were not used as integration inputs.

Exactly one next campaign is recommended: **Remaining L0 Gate Resolution and
Targeted DEV Validation**. Resolve the three explicit business decisions,
provide a disposable native database for Run/conversion acceptance, complete
the existing physical-recovery/blind-sender and V2-only accounting boundary
gaps, then validate landed slices in DEV. Do not begin Storefront or unrelated
L2/L3 work. No new L1 package was opened during this recovery.

Decision requests are deliberately narrow: (1) whether an issued Invoice PDF
remains historical-only or appends a separately identified canonical-current
balance; (2) which structural/capability authority (or both) defines the
protected Team-administrator floor and recovery rule; and (3) the Sales-owned
Portal Quote publication predicate and visibility of last-sent checkpoint versus
unsent revision. Until answered, no implementation invents these semantics.

An independent checkpoint review (`ses_f038bd24effe294e7IgAvM5qpz`) confirmed
the 12/3 dispositions and required three clarifications without changing their
status. For OPS-12, query execution in PGlite and exclusive-membership concurrency
are separate claims; native two-client PostgreSQL contention is required before
asserting the latter. For OPS-13, implementation is limited to exact existing
attempt/work identity and already-frozen route/hold rules; all new successor
lineage/quantity/cancellation/readiness semantics remain gated by BDR-4. For
FUL-11, the billing-address sender default applies only to blind shipment; current
non-blind company/organization sender behavior remains unchanged.

Wave 2 preserves BDR-1 through BDR-4. The L0 triage and immutable-baseline details
are recorded in the reconciliation ledger, which remains coordinator-owned.

## Audit Ownership

All Phase 1 agents are read-only and compare both pinned source trees. Requested
GPT-5.6 Tera was not returned by the model catalog; the permitted fallback is
GPT-6 Astra XHigh for audit/review and subsequent bounded workers.

| Prefix | Scope |
| --- | --- |
| CRM | Customers, Contacts, commercial profiles, activity and CRM row actions |
| SALE | Quotes, Orders, staff sales editing and commercial line workflows |
| PRODDEF | Staff Product/price/recipe/routing definition administration |
| ART | Artwork, Proofing, Prepress and related previews/outputs |

| BILL | Invoices, Statements, commercial PDFs and document/email workflows |

| ACCESS | Existing Customer Portal, Auth, RBAC, Team and Settings |
| UTIL | Dashboard, reporting, global navigation/search and shared daily utilities |

Domain-specific notes/menus are counted under their owner; shared utilities are
not counted again merely because they appear on several screens.

## Classification Rules

One row represents one meaningful operator intent, not each endpoint or button.
Each row receives exactly one A-F classification and one L0-L4 priority.

| Class | Meaning |
| --- | --- |
| A | Equivalent V2 workflow verified with sufficient source/test evidence |
| B | V2 differs intentionally and safely satisfies or improves the intent |
| C | Partial operational workflow |
| D | Required capability materially absent |
| E | Unsafe, obsolete, superseded or intentionally excluded V1 behavior |
| F | Explicit business/ownership decision required before safe implementation |

Priorities: L0 core launch blocker; L1 required daily operation; L2 important with
a bounded workaround; L3 post-launch polish; L4 intentional exclusion. A and B
do not imply live validation. V1 defects are not requirements to reproduce.

Every capability row records domain, intent, V1 behavior, V2 behavior,
classification, priority, source/test evidence, concrete gap/action, owner,
static/automated/local validation, live evidence, confidence and decision notes.
The coordinator adjudicates cross-domain duplicates and conflicting reports.

## Exclusions And Decision Gates

No Customer Ordering Storefront, cart, customer Product catalog, publishing,
checkout, merchandising, themes, promotional codes or online Order placement.
Staff-side capabilities are not relabeled Storefront foundations.

BDR-2 material-source changes, BDR-4 general successor/quantity/cancellation
policy, and payment-release/credit/terms/override policy are F dependencies, not
worker implementation choices. Existing approved nonproduction Fulfillment and
original/replacement separation remain valid.

## First Implementation Wave (Historical)

Coordinator freeze: P1, P2, P3, P4 and P5 are authorized for bounded implementation
after all ten read-only reports and the cross-domain reconciliation returned.
Only the exact existing-contract defects below are selected, not whole domains.
P2's replay/scope contract is resolved below using existing immutable revisions.
No business-decision row is authorized. Final code still
requires independent review, corrections, retests and coordinator integration.

### Frozen Acceptance Contracts

P1/P2/P3/P4/P5 below are the first authorized scope. Each uses
existing authority and changes
neither financial facts nor quantity/release policy. All require independent
nonauthor review of final bytes, focused deterministic regressions and final
canonical validation before integration/push.

| Package | Bounded intent and owner | Acceptance and exclusions |
| --- | --- | --- |
| P1 / ART-04 | Artwork authorization before binary side effects | Actual Artwork owner checks scope, existing `artwork.adopt` capability and matching nonempty request identity before any storage/ledger call. Final adoption/replacement reauthorizes normally. Preserve Portal delegation, replay, validation and cleanup; no new upload format, replacement/successor semantics or storage policy. Denied/mismatched probes must observe zero storage/ledger effects. |
| P2 / FUL-25 | Shipping consumes existing replacement authority | Prepared create/correct/finalize/void must enforce `fulfillment.replace` when the command affects replacement allocations, including removal and existing persisted scope, rather than trusting browser flags. Authorization and scope inspection share the mutation transaction and lock. Exact replay remains freshly authorized; original-only commands still need only their existing grants. No quantity, financial, handoff ownership, downstream lifecycle or provider change. |
| P3 / OPS-15 | Production Run detail authorization | Run detail requires the same `production.view` capability and tenant scope as Run list, before any transaction/read. Actual service tests cover denied/allowed/foreign-tenant calls. No change to run creation/execution, output, holds, successor policy or the OPS-12 SQL query. |
| P4 / UTIL-03 subset | Reporting/UI honest Dashboard read recovery | Distinguish loading, empty permitted categories, legitimate zero, authentic denial and failed service read. Hide prior counts on failed/denied refetch; explicit read-only retry/refresh. Never label client retrieval time canonical snapshot time. Keep Payment Today/Month unchanged. No SQL metric/population changes; UTIL-03 remains partial until owner eligibility/freshness semantics are reconciled. |
| P5 / BILL-22 subset | Billing/Communications email-dialog isolation | Invoice email selection never renders or invokes Payment/Refund/Card controls. Failed recipient preview has an explicit read-only retry retaining the captured selection and email request identity; stale preview must not authorize admission. Mounted tests demonstrate no financial/provider mutation. No financial arithmetic, provider initialization, new composer fields, delivery policy, or claim of full email-recovery parity. |

Independent source-only scope review: `ses_f05f55bf0ffevnJo3zuGyMe9O6`.
This is not final-code acceptance. P2 additionally freezes the following:

- New/resumed create authorizes proposed allocations. Correction authorizes the
  union of the locked active revision and proposed allocations, including removal.
  Finalize/void authorize the locked active revision on the same client/transaction.
- Expose the adapter's existing `get(..., { forUpdate: true })` through the
  transaction contract. Missing support cannot silently skip authorization.
- Replay rechecks the current caller's `fulfillment.ship` and, when affected,
  `fulfillment.replace` before returning a stored result. Preserve request,
  operation, tenant and payload-fingerprint conflict semantics.
- Replay scope uses the saved revision for create/finalize/void. For correction,
  use its saved resulting revision plus its immediate superseded revision through
  a narrow tenant/shipment/revision-scoped owner read. Do not use only today's
  active revision or every historical replacement indiscriminately.
- An exact replacement-removal correction replay requires replacement authority;
  a subsequent new original-only operation does not. Authorized replay repeats
  no mutation, availability check, finalization or lifecycle reconciliation.
- Missing/inconsistent historical evidence fails closed. No new receipt envelope,
  historical rewrite, schema change or grant is authorized. Tests must exercise
  removal/replay/revocation, later corrections, mixed/original allocations,
  foreign scope, changed-payload conflict and lock-before-inspection ordering.

P3 deliberately does not make the broken run-candidate query executable while
OPS-13 run/ordinary-attempt exclusivity and hold invariants remain unresolved.
P5 is not PAY-08/PAY-09 uncertain Payment/refund recovery. The broader read,
financial, publication and workflow gaps remain visible in the matrix.

File ownership is disjoint: P1 Artwork application/upload and its tests; P2
shipment-container application/adapter and its tests; P3 Production Run
application and its tests; P4 Command Center/action-center route and their tests;
P5 Finance workspace and its tests. The coordinator owns all registration,
classification/baseline metadata, audit documentation, integration and Git
commits/pushes. No migration is authorized in this wave.

P5 integration verification additionally owns the existing
`v2/tests/infrastructure/invoiceEmailDeliveryQueue.pure.ts` source contract. Its
old `emailInvoiceIds` variable-name assertion failed after the intent became a
scope/request/selection object. The coordinator replaced that assertion with
three explicit checks for the readonly captured selection and both preview and
admission using it. All provider/queue/ambiguity assertions remain unchanged;
mounted behavior tests remain required. This test-only adjustment requires
independent review and a fresh canonical pass, not an exemption or exclusion.

STATIC means scoped source/contracts and negative guardrails. AUTOMATED TESTS
means the executed deterministic suites above. LOCAL includes actual-service
inert ports and actual SQL in in-memory PGlite, not native multi-client locking.
DEV means Git delivery only in this campaign, not a fresh browser/provider/data
test. MAIN means read-only pinned source comparison; MAIN/PROD remain untouched.
Native disposable-clone rehearsals, new live/provider/physical-print checks and
all V1 tests/builds were not run. Historical live closures retain only their
original exact scope; no ORD-1021 or other QA fixture was changed.

commit, checks fresh DEV ancestry, pushes only that exact SHA to `refs/heads/dev`,
and verifies the remote ref. The final completion report contains that receipt.
No deployment wait/poll or automatic MAIN/PROD promotion is authorized.

## Wave 1 Closeout (Historical)

At Wave 1 closeout, the denominator remained 273. ART-04, FUL-25 and BILL-22
moved C to B. OPS-15 stayed C but moved L0 to L1 after its unauthorized-read
repair, with UI alignment remaining; UTIL-03 stayed C/L1. That historical wave
made no other promotion or business decision. The current scorecard below also
includes the separately recorded Wave 2 additions and transitions.

The raw evidence has 65 file-level source citations without line ranges and 31
rows without a specific test citation. ART-29's unnamed test, PRODDEF-07's
directory-less AI adapter and PRODDEF-16's unkeyed Material page remain explicit
citation limitations, not invented evidence. Counts are not a readiness percentage.

The Wave 1 recommendation was a second safe L0/L1 closure swarm, not Storefront or
blanket live acceptance. Prioritize remaining Sales/Portal publication integrity,
Production Run execution/exclusivity, Finance exact-retry/current-document truth,
and shipment-builder/physical-retry safety. Include targeted completion of the
unassessed Inventory/Inbound coverage before broader readiness conclusions.
Use six bounded parallel lanes with disjoint file ownership or serialized shared
hotspots, GPT-6 Astra XHigh and independent nonauthor review. Policy-dependent
work stays F until accepted contracts exist. Coordinated DEV live validation
follows source closure rather than substituting for these unresolved blockers.
That historical recommendation has now been executed as Wave 2; the single
current next-campaign recommendation is in the recovery outcome above.

### Coverage And Counting Limits

- The denominator covers source-proven intents from the requested ten audit lanes, not all possible application features or a readiness percentage.
- Inventory/Materials has definitions and usage fragments but no complete receiving/reservation/release/adjustment/reconciliation/BD-6 coexistence audit in these reports.
- Inbound ingest/source evidence/review/matching/idempotent submission, Procurement/vendor/PO/receiving, reusable Nesting and general AI workflows were not completely audited by these ten lane reports. No parity class is fabricated for unassessed workflows.
- Source and test inspection are distinct from coordinator baseline execution, new regression execution, in-memory SQL and historical live evidence.
- F counts are decision-dependent capability rows, not the number of independent business decisions. BDR references can occur on several distinct workflows.
- The current-baseline user closures remain bounded; no new live/provider check or fixture mutation is authorized. MAIN/PROD and V1 source remain read-only.
- Storefront, customer catalog/cart/checkout/publishing/themes/promotions and online Order placement remain explicitly excluded. Staff Product version publication is a separate pre-existing domain concept, not Storefront scope.

Whole-row aliases: `ACCESS-19 -> ART-18`; `ACCESS-27 -> UTIL-19`.
Partially overlapping PDF, policy and presentation facets remain under the owning
intent identified in each row's coordinator notes. Raw reports and candidates are
retained in [the inventory](OPERATIONAL_PARITY_2026_10_01.inventory.json); overrides and closure evidence
are retained in [the reconciliation ledger](OPERATIONAL_PARITY_2026_10_01.reconciliation.json).
